# The members' destinations (closure.py, README.md "The destinations"; the ruling on round 12, 1): a line
#   port <symbol or prefix*> <teq function>
# per member a landing ports, its teq function an existing one (`<path>:<name>`) or one to write (`+<name>`), many
# members to one function where a mechanism's helpers fold into it (the table's row for that function enumerates
# them). A member with no line, no `owner` line and no `flag` line is in its landing's unported-<N>.txt.
